- **skill**: Prints a `SKILL.md` file for coding agents that explains how to use `grit`: the JSON output contract, [CLI conventions](../cli-conventions/) and [exit codes](../exit-codes/), everyday commands, copy-paste **Recipes** (CI-tested command sequences), how `grit commit` differs from `git commit`, how to stay non-interactive, and what to do with `git` instead. It uses the [Agent Skills](https://agentski ([gitbutlerapp/grit](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/grit/blob/HEAD/content/docs/commands/skill.md) · ★598)

- **skill**: Print the a2a-cli SKILL.md bundled into this binary: instructions for teaching an agent to drive A2A agents with this CLI. ([a2aproject/a2a-cli](https://github.com/a2aproject/a2a-cli/blob/HEAD/docs/reference/commands/skill.md) · ★197)
- **skill**: &gt; Read this only when the task is to find, install, or create a Production Skill, &gt; or to turn a canvas (current or from history) into a reusable Skill. ([avabbbb/Flovart](https://github.com/avabbbb/Flovart/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/iris/commands/skill.md) · ★146 · 4 copies)
